# Project SEKAI story inventory

Snapshot 2026-10-20. 1,623 readable stories in 188 collections. This list also retains 128 unavailable entries. [All games](../content-inventory.md).

Original Japanese voice clips in all 1,623 readable chapters; individual lines can be unvoiced. No selectable Chinese or English dialogue dub.

Japanese and Simplified Chinese titles for all 1,623 readable stories; English titles for 1,615, with 8 original-title fallbacks.

All 1,623 readable stories have download recipes using the public mirror, on Windows and in the browser. Download before offline reading. Another 128 metadata entries have no available script or recipe.

Original titles are preserved. Verified alternate title languages appear where supplied. "None" under voice means no imported spoken dialogue clips; background music and effects can still be present. Movie soundtracks can contain music without spoken dialogue.

## Coverage limits

- Dialogue is zh-CN only. Japanese and English are catalog title translations; there is no Japanese or English dialogue corpus in these chapters.
- Voices use original Japanese media. This inventory does not claim every passage is voiced.
- All 1,623 readable stories have standalone public-mirror download recipes. Readable-after-download means content is in the application catalog and acquirable, not necessarily installed in the current browser profile.
- Original scene media includes Live2D, backgrounds, music, sound effects, and selected original movies. This does not imply every story contains a movie.
- 33 chapters project original motion onto the native costume rig. Five use original song audio in place of native 3D performance. Five use continuous-score approximation for native music blocks and three for adaptive music controls. Two original video backgrounds use browser loop/mute policy.
- The 128 unavailable-source HTTP results were checked at 2026-10-19T10:14:07.264Z. No network availability retest was performed for this inventory.
- Metadata-only entries are not present in the normal published readable application catalog.
